float HLGInverseOETF(float x) { 
   if (x > 1.0 / 2.0) { x = (exp((x - 0.55991073) / 0.17883277) + 0.28466892) / 12.0; }  
   else {  x = pow(x, 2.0) / 3.0; }  
   return x;

   return gain * pow(y, gam - 1.0) * x;
float HLGEOTF(float x, vec3 src) { 
   float Lw = 100.0; float Lb = 0.005; float gain = 1.0; 
   float gam = 1.2 + 0.42 * log(Lw / 1000.0) / log(10.0);  
   float p = sqrt(3.0 * pow(Lb / Lw, 1.0 / gam)); 
   float y = 0.2627 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.r + p)) + 0.678 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.g + p)) + 0.0593 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.b + p)); 
   return HLGOOTF(HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * x + p)), y, gam, gain);

float clamper(float v) {return min(1.0, max(0.0, v));} 
float BT709OETF(float x) { 
   if (x < 0.018) { x = x * 4.5; } 
   else {  x = 1.099 * pow(x, 0.45) - 0.099; } 
   return x; 
float HLGInverseOETF(float x) { 
   if (x > 1.0 / 2.0) { x = (exp((x - 0.55991073) / 0.17883277) + 0.28466892) / 12.0; }  
   else {  x = pow(x, 2.0) / 3.0; }  
   return x; 
 float HLGOOTF(float x, float y, float gam, float gain) { 
   return gain * pow(y, gam - 1.0) * x;
float HLGEOTF(float x, vec3 src) { 
   float Lw = 100.0; float Lb = 0.005; float gain = 1.0; 
   float gam = 1.2 + 0.42 * log(Lw / 1000.0) / log(10.0);  
   float p = sqrt(3.0 * pow(Lb / Lw, 1.0 / gam)); 
   float y = 0.2627 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.r + p)) + 0.678 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.g + p)) + 0.0593 * HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * src.b + p)); 
   return HLGOOTF(HLGInverseOETF(max(0.0, (1.0 - p) * x + p)), y, gam, gain); 
void main(){                                            
   mat3 mTransform = mat3(1.66049051, -0.124550462, -0.0181507654, -0.58764118, 1.1329, -0.100578845, -0.0728498995, -0.00834938883, 1.11872947); 
   vec4 color = texture2D(uTexture0, vTexCoord);      
   vec3 src; vec3 temp; 
   color.r = clamper(color.r); 
   color.g = clamper(color.g); 
   color.b = clamper(color.b); 
   src.r = color.r; 
   src.g = color.g; 
   src.b = color.b; 
   temp.r = HLGEOTF(color.r, src); temp.g = HLGEOTF(color.g, src); temp.b = HLGEOTF(color.b, src); 
   temp = mTransform * temp; 
   temp.r = BT709OETF(clamper(temp.r)); temp.g = BT709OETF(clamper(temp.g)); temp.b = BT709OETF(clamper(temp.b)); 
   vec4 dst; 
   dst.a = color.a; 
   dst.r = clamper(temp.r); 
   dst.g = clamper(temp.g); 
   dst.b = clamper(temp.b); 
   gl_FragColor = dst;

float BT709EOTF(float x) { 
   if (x < 0.081) { x = x / 4.5; } 
   else {  x = pow((x + 0.099) / 1.099, 1.0 / 0.45); } 
   return x; 
float BT709OETF(float x) { 
   if (x < 0.018) { x = x * 4.5; } 
   else {  x = 1.099 * pow(x, 0.45) - 0.099; } 
   return x;

precision mediump float;
#else
#define highp

varying highp vec2 vTexCoord;                           
uniform sampler2D uTexture0;                             
float clamper(float v) {return min(1.0, max(0.0, v));} 
float DisplayP3EOTF(float x) { 
   if (x < 0.0390000008) { x = x * 0.0773993805; } 
   else {  x = pow((0.947867333 * x + 0.0521327034), 2.4000001); } 
   return x; 
float BT709OETF(float x) { 
   if (x < 0.018) { x = x * 4.5; } 
   else {  x = 1.099 * pow(x, 0.45) - 0.099; } 
   return x; 
void main(){                                            
   mat3 mTransform = mat3(1.224940062, -0.042056948, -0.019637555, -0.224940300, 1.042057037, -0.078635968, 0.000000000, 0.000000015, 1.098273396); 
   vec4 color = texture2D(uTexture0, vTexCoord);      
   vec3 temp;
   temp.r = DisplayP3EOTF(color.r);
   temp.g = DisplayP3EOTF(color.g);
   temp.b = DisplayP3EOTF(color.b);
   temp = mTransform * temp; 
   temp.r = BT709OETF(temp.r);
   temp.g = BT709OETF(temp.g);
   temp.b = BT709OETF(temp.b);
   vec4 dst; 
   dst.a = color.a; 
   dst.r = clamper(temp.r); 
   dst.g = clamper(temp.g); 
   dst.b = clamper(temp.b); 
   gl_FragColor = dst;
